   126: void FriendListRequest(int aIndex)
   127: {
   128: 	if ( gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == FALSE )
   129: 	{
   130: 		return;
   131: 	}
   132: 
   133: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST_REQ pMsg;
   134: 
   135: 	pMsg.h.set(0x60, sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST_REQ));
   136: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, gObj[aIndex].Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   137: 	pMsg.Number = aIndex;
   138: 	pMsg.pServer = (BYTE)gServerInfo.m_ServerCode;
   139: 
   140: 	CSDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   141: }
   142: 
   143: void FriendListResult(LPBYTE lpMsg)
   144: {
   145: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT * lpCount;
   146: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST * lpList;
   147: 	int lOfs = sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT);
   148: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT pCount;
   149: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST pList;
   150: 	int pOfs = sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT);
   151: 
   152: 	lpCount = (FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT *)lpMsg;
   153: 
   154: 	BYTE TmpSendBuf[2000];
   155: 
   156: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpCount->Number, lpCount->Name))
   157: 	{
   158: 		return;
   159: 	}
   160: 
   161: 	pCount.Count = lpCount->Count;
   162: 	pCount.MemoCount = lpCount->MailCount;
   163: 	pCount.MailTotal = 50;
   164: 
   165: 	if ( pCount.Count != 0 )
   166: 	{
   167: 		for ( int n=0;n<pCount.Count;n++)
   168: 		{
   169: 			lpList = (FHP_FRIENDLIST *)&lpMsg[lOfs];
   170: 
   171: 			memcpy(pList.Name,lpList->Name,sizeof(pList.Name));
   172: 
   173: 			pList.Server = lpList->Server;
   174: 
   175: 			memcpy(&TmpSendBuf[pOfs], &pList, sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST));
   176: 
   177: 			lOfs+=sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST);
   178: 			pOfs+=sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST);
   179: 		}
   180: 	}
   181: 
   182: 	pCount.h.set(0xC0, pOfs);
   183: 	memcpy(TmpSendBuf, &pCount, sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT));
   184: 
   185: 	DataSend(lpCount->Number, TmpSendBuf, pOfs);
   186: }

   188: void WaitFriendListResult(FHP_WAITFRIENDLIST_COUNT * lpMsg)
   189: {
   190: 	PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_SIN_REQ pMsg;
   191: 
   192: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpMsg->Number, lpMsg->Name))
   193: 	{
   194: 		return;
   195: 	}
   196: 
   197: 	pMsg.h.set(0xC2, sizeof(pMsg));

   235: void FriendAddRequest(PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_REQ * lpMsg, int aIndex)
   236: {
   237: 	if ( !gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex))
   238: 	{
   239: 		return;
   240: 	}
   241: 
   242: 	//Season 4.5 Addon start
   243: 	LPOBJ find = gObjFind(lpMsg->Name);
   244: 
   245: 	int aTargetIndex = ((find==0)?-1:find->Index);
   246: 
   247:     if(aTargetIndex >= 0 && aTargetIndex <= MAX_OBJECT-1 && gObj[aTargetIndex].Type != 1  )
   248:     {
   249: 		CloseClient(aIndex);
   250: 		return;
   251: 	}
   252: 	//Season 4.5 Addon end
   253: 
   254: 	FHP_FRIEND_ADD_REQ pMsg;
   255: 
   256: 	pMsg.h.set(0x63, sizeof(pMsg));
   257: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, gObj[aIndex].Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   258: 	memset(pMsg.FriendName, 0 , sizeof(pMsg.FriendName));
   259: 	memcpy(pMsg.FriendName, lpMsg->Name, sizeof(lpMsg->Name));
   260: 	pMsg.Number = aIndex;
   261: 
   262: 	char szName[11];
   263: 
   264: 	memset(szName,0,sizeof(szName));
   265: 
   266: 	memcpy(szName,lpMsg->Name,sizeof(lpMsg->Name));
   267: 
   268: 	if ( !strcmp(gObj[aIndex].Name, szName))
   269: 	{
   270: 		PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_RESULT pResult;
   271: 
   272: 		pResult.h.set(0xC1, sizeof(pResult));
   273: 		memcpy(pResult.Name, lpMsg->Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   274: 		pResult.Result = 5;
   275: 		pResult.State = -1;
   276: 
   277: 		DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pResult, sizeof(pResult));
   278: 	}
   279: 	else
   280: 	{
   281: 		CSDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   282: 	}
   283: }

   356: void FriendDelResult(FHP_FRIEND_DEL_RESULT * lpMsg)
   357: {
   358: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpMsg->Number, lpMsg->Name))
   359: 	{
   360: 		return;
   361: 	}
   362: 
   363: 	PMSG_FRIEND_DEL_RESULT pMsg;
   364: 
   365: 	pMsg.h.set(0xC3, sizeof(pMsg));
   366: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, lpMsg->FriendName, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   367: 	pMsg.Result = lpMsg->Result;
   368: 
   369: 	DataSend(lpMsg->Number, (LPBYTE)&pMsg, sizeof(pMsg));
   370: }
   371: 
   372: void FriendChatRoomCreateReq(PMSG_FRIEND_ROOMCREATE_REQ * lpMsg, int aIndex)
   373: {
   374: 	if ( !gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex))
   375: 	{
   376: 		return;
   377: 	}
   378: 
```

    15: void CComboSkill::Init() // OK
    16: {
    17: 	this->m_time = 0;
    18: 	this->m_skill[0] = 0xFFFF;
    19: 	this->m_skill[1] = 0xFFFF;
    20: 	this->m_index = -1;
    21: }
    22: 
    23: int CComboSkill::GetSkillType(WORD skill) // OK
    24: {
    25: 	if (gServerInfo.m_EnableComboToAllSwitch == 1)
    26: 	{
    27: 		if(gCustomCombo.CheckSkillPrimary(skill) == 1)
    28: 		{
    29: 			return 0;
    30: 		}
    31: 		else if(gCustomCombo.CheckSkillSecundary(skill) == 1)
    32: 		{
    33: 			return 1;
    34: 		}
    35: 		else
    36: 		{
    37: 			return -1;
    38: 		}
    39: 	}
    40: 	else
    41: 	{
    42: 		if(skill == SKILL_FALLING_SLASH || skill == SKILL_LUNGE || skill == SKILL_UPPERCUT || skill == SKILL_CYCLONE || skill == SKILL_SLASH)
    43: 		{
    44: 			return 0;
    45: 		}
    46: 		else if(skill == SKILL_TWISTING_SLASH || skill == SKILL_RAGEFUL_BLOW || skill == SKILL_DEATH_STAB || skill == SKILL_FROZEN_STAB || skill == SKILL_BLOOD_STORM)
    47: 		{
    48: 			return 1;
    49: 		}
    50: 		else
    51: 		{
    52: 			return -1;
    53: 		}
    54: 	}
    55: }
    56: 
    57: bool CComboSkill::CheckCombo(WORD skill) // OK
    58: {
    59: 	int type = this->GetSkillType(skill);
    60: 
    61: 	if(type == -1)
    62: 	{
    63: 		this->Init();
    64: 		return 0;
    65: 	}
    66: 
    67: 	if(type == 0)
    68: 	{
    69: 		this->m_time = GetTickCount()+3000;
    70: 		this->m_skill[0] = skill;
    71: 		this->m_index = 0;
    72: 		return 0;
    73: 	}
    74: 
    75: 	if(type == 1)
    76: 	{
    77: 		if(this->m_time < GetTickCount())
    78: 		{
    79: 			this->Init();
    80: 			return 0;
    81: 		}
    82: 
    83: 		if(this->m_skill[0] == 0xFFFF)
    84: 		{
    85: 			this->Init();
    86: 			return 0;
    87: 		}
    88: 
    89: 		if(this->m_index == 0)
    90: 		{
    91: 			this->m_time = GetTickCount()+3000;
    92: 			this->m_skill[1] = skill;
    93: 			this->m_index = 1;
    94: 			return 0;
    95: 		}
    96: 
    97: 		if(this->m_index == 1 && this->m_skill[1] != skill)
    98: 		{
    99: 			this->Init();
   100: 			return 1;
   101: 		}
   102: 	}
   103: 
   104: 	this->Init();
   105: 	return 0;
   106: }

  1006: void DGCharacterCreateRecv(SDHP_CHARACTER_CREATE_RECV* lpMsg) // OK
  1007: {
  1008: 	if(gObjIsAccountValid(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account) == 0)
  1009: 	{
  1010: 		LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[DGCharacterCreateRecv] Invalid Account [%d](%s)",lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account);
  1011: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
  1012: 		return;
  1013: 	}
  1014: 
  1015: 	PMSG_CHARACTER_CREATE_SEND pMsg;
  1016: 
  1017: 	pMsg.header.set(0xF3,0x01,sizeof(pMsg));
  1018: 
  1019: 	pMsg.result = lpMsg->result;
  1020: 
  1021: 	memcpy(pMsg.name,lpMsg->name,sizeof(pMsg.name));
  1022: 
  1023: 	pMsg.slot = lpMsg->slot;
  1024: 
  1025: 	pMsg.level = lpMsg->level;
  1026: 
  1027: 	pMsg.Class = (lpMsg->Class%16)*16;
  1028: 	pMsg.Class -= (pMsg.Class/32);
  1029: 	pMsg.Class += (lpMsg->Class/16)*32;
  1030: 
  1031: 	memcpy(pMsg.equipment,lpMsg->equipment,sizeof(pMsg.equipment));
  1032: 
  1033: 	DataSend(lpMsg->index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
  1034: }

  1054: void DGCharacterInfoRecv(SDHP_CHARACTER_INFO_RECV* lpMsg) // OK
  1055: {
  1056: 	if(gObjIsAccountValid(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account) == 0)
  1057: 	{
  1058: 		LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[DGCharacterInfoRecv] Invalid Account [%d](%s)",lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account);
  1059: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
  1060: 		return;
  1061: 	}
  1062: 
  1063: 	if(lpMsg->result == 0)
  1064: 	{
  1065: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
  1066: 		return;
  1067: 	}
  1068: 
  1069: 	if((lpMsg->CtlCode & 1) != 0)
  1070: 	{
  1071: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
  1072: 		return;
  1073: 	}
  1074: 
  1075: 	if(gObjectManager.CharacterInfoSet((BYTE*)lpMsg,lpMsg->index) == 0)
  1076: 	{
  1077: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
  1078: 		return;
  1079: 	}
  1080: 
  1081: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[lpMsg->index];
  1082: 
  1083: 	if(lpObj->MapServerMoveRequest == 0)
  1084: 	{
  1085: 		if((lpObj->NextServerCode=gMapServerManager.CheckMapServerMove(lpObj->Index,lpObj->Map,lpObj->LastServerCode)) != gServerInfo.m_ServerCode)
  1086: 		{
  1087: 			if(lpObj->NextServerCode == -1)
  1088: 			{
  1089: 				CloseClient(lpObj->Index);
  1090: 				return;
  1091: 			}
  1092: 			else
  1093: 			{
  1094: 				GJMapServerMoveSend(lpObj->Index,lpObj->NextServerCode,lpObj->Map,(BYTE)lpObj->X,(BYTE)lpObj->Y);
  1095: 				return;
  1096: 			}
  1097: 		}
  1098: 	}
  1099: 
  1100: 	lpObj->MapMoveDisable = 1;
  1101: 
  1102: 	PMSG_CHARACTER_INFO_SEND pMsg;
  1103: 
  1104: 	pMsg.header.set(0xF3,0x03,sizeof(pMsg));
  1105: 
  1106: 	pMsg.X = (BYTE)lpObj->X;
  1107: 	pMsg.Y = (BYTE)lpObj->Y;
  1108: 	pMsg.Map = lpObj->Map;
  1109: 	pMsg.Dir = lpObj->Dir;
  1110: 
  1111: 	if(gMasterSkillTree.CheckMasterLevel(lpObj) == 0)
  1112: 	{
  1113: 		pMsg.Experience[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1114: 		pMsg.Experience[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1115: 		pMsg.Experience[2] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1116: 		pMsg.Experience[3] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1117: 		pMsg.Experience[4] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1118: 		pMsg.Experience[5] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1119: 		pMsg.Experience[6] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1120: 		pMsg.Experience[7] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->Experience)));
  1121: 	}
  1122: 	else
  1123: 	{
  1124: 		pMsg.Experience[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1125: 		pMsg.Experience[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1126: 		pMsg.Experience[2] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1127: 		pMsg.Experience[3] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1128: 		pMsg.Experience[4] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1129: 		pMsg.Experience[5] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1130: 		pMsg.Experience[6] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1131: 		pMsg.Experience[7] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->MasterExperience)));
  1132: 	}
  1133: 
  1134: 	if(gMasterSkillTree.CheckMasterLevel(lpObj) == 0)
  1135: 	{
  1136: 		pMsg.NextExperience[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->NextExperience)));
  1137: 		pMsg.NextExperience[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERHW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->NextExperience)));
  1138: 		pMsg.NextExperience[2] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERHDW(lpObj->NextExperience)));

  1142: 		pMsg.NextExperience[6] = SET_NUMBERHB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->NextExperience)));

  1154: 		pMsg.NextExperience[7] = SET_NUMBERLB(SET_NUMBERLW(SET_NUMBERLDW(lpObj->MasterNextExperience)));
  1155: 	}

    96: bool CHackSkillCheck::GetInfo(int index,HACKSKILL_INFO* lpInfo) // OK

   111: bool CHackSkillCheck::GetInfoBySpeed(int Skill,int Speed, HACKSKILL_INFO* lpInfo) // OK
   112: {
   113: 	for(std::map<int,HACKSKILL_INFO>::iterator it=this->m_HackSkillInfo.begin();it != this->m_HackSkillInfo.end();it++)
   114: 	{
   115: 		if(it->second.Skill == Skill && Speed >= it->second.MinSpeed && (Speed <= it->second.MaxSpeed || it->second.MaxSpeed == -1))
   116: 		{
   117: 			(*lpInfo) = it->second;
   118: 			return 1;
   119: 		}
   120: 	}
   121: 
   122: 	return 0;
   123: }
   124: 
   125: bool CHackSkillCheck::CheckSpeedHack(LPOBJ lpObj,int skill) // OK
   126: {
   127: 	if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillEnable != 1)
   128: 	{
   129: 		return 0;
   130: 	}
   131: 
   132: 	HACKSKILL_INFO HackSkillInfo;
   133: 
   134: 	if(this->GetInfoBySpeed(skill,lpObj->PhysiSpeed,&HackSkillInfo) == 0)
   135: 	{
   136: 		return 0;
   137: 	}
   138: 
   139: 	if(lpObj->SpeedHackSkill != skill)
   140: 	{
   141: 		lpObj->SpeedHackSkill = skill;
   142: 		lpObj->SpeedHackCount = 0;
   143: 		lpObj->SpeedHackDelay = GetTickCount();
   144: 		lpObj->SpeedHackDialog = 0;
   145: 	}
   146: 
   147: 	if(lpObj->SpeedHackCount > HackSkillInfo.MaxCount)
   148: 	{
   149: 
   150: 		if( lpObj->SpeedHackDialog == 0)
   151: 		{
   152: 			LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[SpeedHackDetected][%s][%s] Skill: %d",lpObj->Account,lpObj->Name,skill);
   153: 
   154: 			if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillDialog == 1)
   155: 			{
   156: 				gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(843));
   157: 			}
   158: 			lpObj->SpeedHackDialog = 1;
   159: 		}
   160: 
   161: 		if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillType == 1)
   162: 		{
   163: 			GCCloseClientSend(lpObj->Index,0);
   164: 			return 1;
   165: 		}
   166: 		else if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillType == 2)
   167: 		{
   168: 			return 1;
   169: 		}
   170: 		else if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillType == 3)
   171: 		{
   172: 			if( GetTickCount() > (lpObj->SpeedHackDelay + HackSkillInfo.Delay + (DWORD)gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillPenalty))
   173: 			{
   174: 				lpObj->SpeedHackCount = 0;
   175: 				lpObj->SpeedHackDelay = GetTickCount();
   176: 				lpObj->SpeedHackDialog = 0;
   177: 			}
   178: 			return 1;
   179: 		}
   180: 		else if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillType == 4)
   181: 		{
   182: 			SDHP_COMMAND_BLOC_SEND pMsg;
   183: 
   184: 			pMsg.header.set(0x0F,0x06,sizeof(pMsg));
   185: 
   186: 			pMsg.index = lpObj->Index;
   187: 
   188: 			pMsg.days = 30;
   189: 
   190: 			memcpy(pMsg.namebloc,lpObj->Account,sizeof(pMsg.namebloc));
   191: 
   192: 			gDataServerConnection.DataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   193: 
   194: 			gObjUserKill(lpObj->Index);
   195: 			return 1;
   196: 		}
   197: 		else
   198: 		{
   199: 			if( GetTickCount() > (lpObj->SpeedHackDelay+HackSkillInfo.Delay))
   200: 			{
   201: 				lpObj->SpeedHackCount = 0;
   202: 				lpObj->SpeedHackDelay = GetTickCount();
   203: 			}
   204: 		}
   205: 		return 0;
   206: 	}
   207: 
   208: 	if( GetTickCount() < (lpObj->SpeedHackDelay+HackSkillInfo.Delay))
   209: 	{
   210: 		lpObj->SpeedHackCount++;
   211: 		return 0;
   212: 	}
   213: 	else
   214: 	{
   215: 		if (gServerInfo.m_SpeedHackSkillDebug == 1)
   216: 		{
   217: 			LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[SpeedHackTeste] Skill: %d | AttackSpeed: %d | Count: %d",skill,lpObj->PhysiSpeed,lpObj->SpeedHackCount);
   218: 		}
   219: 	}
   220: 
   221: 	lpObj->SpeedHackCount = 0;
   222: 	lpObj->SpeedHackDelay = GetTickCount();
   223: 
   224: 	return 0;
   225: }

  1444: 		#endif
  1445: 	}

  1447: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_VIEWPORT;n++)

    22: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0xD7
    23: #elif(GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE==3)
    24: #define PROTOCOL_CODE1 0xD9
    25: #define PROTOCOL_CODE2 0xD7
    26: #define PROTOCOL_CODE3 0xD0
    27: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0x1D
    28: #elif(GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE==4)
    29: #define PROTOCOL_CODE1 0x00
    30: #define PROTOCOL_CODE2 0x00
    31: #define PROTOCOL_CODE3 0x00
    32: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0x00
    33: #elif(GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE==5)
    34: #define PROTOCOL_CODE1 0xD6
    35: #define PROTOCOL_CODE2 0xDD
    36: #define PROTOCOL_CODE3 0xDF
    37: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0xD2
    38: #elif(GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE==6)
    39: #define PROTOCOL_CODE1 0xDD
    40: #define PROTOCOL_CODE2 0xD6
    41: #define PROTOCOL_CODE3 0xDF
    42: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0x11
    43: #elif(GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE==7)
    44: #define PROTOCOL_CODE1 0xD9
    45: #define PROTOCOL_CODE2 0x15
    46: #define PROTOCOL_CODE3 0xDC
    47: #define PROTOCOL_CODE4 0x1D
    48: #endif
    49: 
    50: // ============================================================
    51: // Compile-time validation: PROTOCOL_CODE must match client
    52: // Client uses: PACKET_MOVE=0xD4, PACKET_ATTACK=0x11, PACKET_POSITION=0x15
    53: // ============================================================
    54: #if GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE == 1
    55: static_assert(PROTOCOL_CODE1 == 0xD4, "PROTOCOL_CODE1 mismatch: client expects 0xD4 (PACKET_MOVE)");
    56: static_assert(PROTOCOL_CODE2 == 0x11, "PROTOCOL_CODE2 mismatch: client expects 0x11 (PACKET_ATTACK)");
    57: static_assert(PROTOCOL_CODE3 == 0x15, "PROTOCOL_CODE3 mismatch: client expects 0x15 (PACKET_POSITION)");
    58: static_assert(PROTOCOL_CODE4 == 0xDB, "PROTOCOL_CODE4 mismatch with client");
    59: #elif GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE != 1
    60: #pragma message("WARNING: GAMESERVER_LANGUAGE is not 1 - PROTOCOL_CODE may not match client!")
    61: #pragma message("WARNING: Client uses PACKET_MOVE=0xD4, PACKET_ATTACK=0x11, PACKET_POSITION=0x15")
    62: #endif
    63: 
    64: #define SET_NUMBERHB(x) ((BYTE)((DWORD)(x)>>(DWORD)8))
    65: #define SET_NUMBERLB(x) ((BYTE)((DWORD)(x)&0xFF))
    66: #define SET_NUMBERHW(x) ((WORD)((DWORD)(x)>>(DWORD)16))
    67: #define SET_NUMBERLW(x) ((WORD)((DWORD)(x)&0xFFFF))
    68: #define SET_NUMBERHDW(x) ((DWORD)((QWORD)(x)>>(QWORD)32))
    69: #define SET_NUMBERLDW(x) ((DWORD)((QWORD)(x)&0xFFFFFFFF))
    70: 
    71: #define MAKE_NUMBERW(x,y) ((WORD)(((BYTE)((y)&0xFF))|((BYTE)((x)&0xFF)<<8)))
    72: #define MAKE_NUMBERDW(x,y) ((DWORD)(((WORD)((y)&0xFFFF))|((WORD)((x)&0xFFFF)<<16)))
    73: #define MAKE_NUMBERQW(x,y) ((QWORD)(((DWORD)((y)&0xFFFFFFFF))|((DWORD)((x)&0xFFFFFFFF)<<32)))
    74: 
    75: //**********************************************//
    76: //************ Packet Base *********************//
    77: //**********************************************//
    78: 
    79: struct PBMSG_HEAD
    80: {
    81: 	void set(BYTE head,BYTE size) // OK
    82: 	{
    83: 		this->type = 0xC1;
    84: 		this->size = size;
    85: 		this->head = head;
    86: 	}
    87: 
    88: 	void setE(BYTE head,BYTE size) // OK
    89: 	{
    90: 		this->type = 0xC3;
    91: 		this->size = size;
    92: 		this->head = head;
    93: 	}
    94: 
    95: 	BYTE type;
    96: 	BYTE size;
    97: 	BYTE head;
    98: };

   583: void CServerInfo::ReadMapInfo() // OK
   584: {
   585: 	FILE* file;
   586: 
   587: 	char path[256] = {0};
   588: 
   589: 	char temp[256] = {0};
   590: 
   591: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_MAP;n++)
   592: 	{
   593: 		wsprintf(temp,"Terrain\\terrain%d.att",(n+1));
   594: 
   595: 		if(fopen_s(&file,gPath.GetFullPath(temp),"r") == 0)
   596: 		{
   597: 			strcpy_s(path,temp);
   598: 			fclose(file);
   599: 		}
   600: 
   601: 		gMap[n].Load(gPath.GetFullPath(path),n);
   602: 	}

   757: void CServerInfo::ReloadMonsterInfo() // OK
   758: {
   759: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_OBJECT_MONSTER;n++)
   760: 	{
   761: 		if(gObj[n].Type != OBJECT_MONSTER && gObj[n].Type != OBJECT_NPC)
   762: 		{
   763: 			continue;
   764: 		}
   765: 
   766: 		#if(GAMESERVER_TYPE==1)
   767: 
   768: 		if(gObj[n].CsNpcType != 0)
   769: 		{
   770: 			continue;
   771: 		}
   772: 
   773: 		#endif
   774: 
   775: 		gObjDel(n);
   776: 	}
   777: 
   778: 	gObjMonCount = 0;
   779: 
   780: 	gCrywolf.m_ObjCommonNPC.Reset();
   781: 
   782: 	gCrywolf.m_ObjSpecialNPC.Reset();
   783: 
   784: 	gCrywolf.m_ObjCommonMonster.Reset();
   785: 
   786: 	gCrywolf.m_ObjSpecialMonster.Reset();
   787: 
   788: 	this->ReadMonsterInfo();
   789: 
   790: 	gMonsterManager.SetMonsterData();
   791: }

   776: 
   777: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SKILL_LIST;n++)
   778: 	{
   779: 		if(lpObj->Skill[n].IsSkill() == 0)
   780: 		{
   781: 			return ((lpObj->Skill[n].Set(index,level)==0)?-1:n);
   782: 		}
   783: 	}
   784: 
   785: 	return -1;
   786: }
   787: 
   788: int CSkillManager::DelSkill(LPOBJ lpObj,int index) // OK
   789: {
   790: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SKILL_LIST;n++)
   791: 	{
   792: 		if(lpObj->Skill[n].IsSkill() != 0)
   793: 		{
   794: 			if(lpObj->Skill[n].m_index == index)
   795: 			{
   796: 				lpObj->Skill[n].Clear();
   797: 				return n;
   798: 			}
   799: 		}
```
